# What H.R. 8398 would do to existing law

> **Derived, unofficial, and not law.** This file is generated from the
> bill's own amendatory instructions. It is not published by any
> government body, it has not been reviewed, and it is wrong wherever
> the instruction was more subtle than the reading of it. Nothing here
> should be relied on; read `bill.md` beside it, and the US Code for the
> text being amended.

2,558 amendatory instructions. 2 executed, 2,556 stated and not applied.

An instruction is executed here only when the bill states **both** the
text removed and the text inserted, so the result follows from this
document alone. Where a bill says *strike subsection (k)*, the words it
removes are in the US Code and not in the bill, and no attempt is made
to guess them.

## Executed

### 19 U.S.C. § 1332

> Section 3(b)(1) of the American Manufacturing Competitiveness Act of 2016 (Public Law 114–159; 19 U.S.C. 1332 note) is amended, in the matter preceding subparagraph (A), by striking “October 15, 2016, and October 15, 2019” and inserting “October 15, 2025, and October 15, 2028”.

| | |
|---|---|
| Removed | `October 15, 2016, and October 15, 2019` |
| Inserted | `October 15, 2025, and October 15, 2028` |

### 19 U.S.C. § 1332

> by striking “12 months” and all that follows through “tariff bill” and inserting “the date that is 20 months after the date of the enactment of a miscellaneous tariff bill or the date on which the duty suspensions and reductions included in the miscellaneous tariff bill take effect, whichever occurs later”; and

| | |
|---|---|
| Removed | `12 months” and all that follows through “tariff bill` |
| Inserted | `the date that is 20 months after the date of the enactment of a miscellaneous tariff bill or the date on which the duty suspensions and reductions included in the miscellaneous tariff bill take effect, whichever occurs later` |
